Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a universe algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 965. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 585/1.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the universe decomposition. By minimality, the element a^965 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 585-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
